To support this task, we construct SurgTension, the first expert-annotated tissue tension dataset, providing a benchmark for objective tissue tension recognition. We further propose TensionTRAC, a lightweight trajectory-based framework that models tissue tension from sparse point trajectories. Using a compact trajectory encoder, TensionTRAC achieves competitive performance against strong pretrained video backbones.

</details>


### [77] [Cross-Domain Generalization in Machine Unlearning via Label-Conditioned Energy Magnitude Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17942)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Syed Bilal Ahsan,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器学习中遗忘某个类别时对模型其他部分的影响。使用标签条件能量模型(EBM)，通过提高目标类别的能量来实现遗忘，并引入传播项将遗忘信号传递给与被遗忘类相似的保留样本。在DomainNet和CIFAR-10数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数机器学习方法将被遗忘的概念视为孤立的存在，忽略了遗忘一个类别对模型其他部分的影响。作者认为需要研究当一个类别被遗忘时，模型其余部分会发生什么变化。

Method: 使用标签条件能量模型(EBM)，通过以下机制实现遗忘：1)提高被遗忘类别的图像-标签对的能量；2)使用遗忘项和保留锚点保持模型性能；3)全局边际约束；4)能量正则化器防止能量无限增长；5)传播项根据样本与被遗忘类的DINOv2相似度，将遗忘信号传递给相似样本。

Result: 1) DomainNet上：在四个视觉域(sketch, real, clipart, painting)上遗忘tiger、lion、scissors。sketch域的遗忘会传播到其他域，lion和scissors的遗忘错误率达到98%和99%，效果还传播到最相似的类别。2) CIFAR-10上：关闭传播项后遗忘每个类别，遗忘完成度达100%，其他九个类别平均保留了98.5%的原始准确率。

Conclusion: 基于能量模型的机器遗忘方法能够有效地选择性遗忘特定类别，同时保持其他类别的性能。传播机制能使遗忘效果扩展到视觉上相似的类别，实现更彻底的遗忘。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific data from a trained model. However, most methods treat the forgotten concept as isolated. In this paper, we study what happens to the rest of the model when a class is forgotten, using a label-conditioned energy-based model (EBM) that assigns per-class energies, making the effect directly observable. We forget a class by raising the energy of its image-label pairs, training with a forget term, a retain anchor to the pretrained model, a global margin, and an energy regularizer that stops the energy magnitudes from growing without limit. A propagation term applies the same forget signal to retain samples, weighted by each sample's DINOv2 similarity to the forget class, so forgetting reaches images that resemble it and leaves the rest untouched. We evaluate on two benchmark datasets: 1) On a subset of DomainNet across four visual domains, we forget tiger, lion, and scissors one at a time. Forgetting a class in the sketch domain also erases it from real, clipart, and painting, with forgetting error reaching 98% and 99% for lion and scissors, and the effect carrying over to the most similar class. 2) On CIFAR-10, we turn off the propagation term and forget each of the ten classes on its own. Forgetting is complete (100%), while the other nine classes retain 98.5% of their pre-unlearning accuracy on average.

</details>


### [78] [SFMformer: A Spatial-Frequency Modulation Transformer for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17966)
*Chih-Hsiang Yang,Chia-Min Lin,Ching-Yu Tsai,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMformer，一种用于轻量级图像超分辨率的稀疏注意力Transformer，通过在稀疏注意力前后分别引入空间增强和频域调制模块来分别提升选择质量和聚合质量，两种改进在缓解不同约束时会产生协同增效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力机制虽高效，但稀疏化改变了网络改进的含义——密集注意力只有一个关注点（聚合），而稀疏注意力有两个（选择和聚合），被丢弃的token无法恢复。因此需要分别针对选择质量和聚合质量进行优化。

Method: 将双分支空间增强模块置于渐进聚焦注意力的输入端， wavelet域调制模块置于其输出端，形成SFMformer。每块启用一次频域调制而非每层一次以降低成本。

Result: 在15个基准测试对上，9个测试对的联合增益超过各自增益之和，弱模块贡献与差异符号呈负相关(r=-0.72)。模型在30个PSNR/SSIM指标中28个排名第一，参数控制在百万以下。

Conclusion: SFMformer在轻量级图像超分辨率任务中表现最优，两模块在缓解不同约束时产生协同效应，为稀疏注意力网络的模块化设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sparse attention mechanisms, which score all token pairs but propagate only the strongest, now underpin the most efficient Transformers for lightweight image super-resolution. This paper observes that sparsification changes what it means to improve such a network. A dense attention layer has one place where representation quality matters: the aggregation of attended features. A sparse layer has two, because the top-k operator first decides which tokens survive and only then decides what to do with them, and a token discarded at the selection stage cannot be recovered downstream. Selection quality and aggregation quality are therefore separable targets, addressed by modules placed before and after the attention respectively. We test this by pairing a dual-branch spatial enhancement on the input of a progressive focused attention with a wavelet-domain modulation on its output, forming SFMformer. Measuring each module alone and jointly over all fifteen benchmark-scale pairs, we find their gains are not additive: the joint gain exceeds the sum of the individual gains on nine pairs, and the sign of the discrepancy is predicted by how much the weaker module contributes on its own (r = -0.72), so the two compound when they relieve different constraints and overlap when they relieve the same one. Enabling spectral modulation once per block rather than once per layer retains the effect at roughly one-sixth of its cost, keeping the model below one million parameters at every scale. SFMformer ranks first on 28 of 30 PSNR/SSIM entries across five benchmarks and three upscaling factors. We report the cases where the pairing does not help, and deploy the model on a Raspberry Pi 5 to confirm the design is practical under tight resource budgets.

</details>


### [79] [LinCa: Accelerating Diffusion Models via Learnable Decomposed Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17973)
*Jinshan Liu,Haoran Qin,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Jiahui Hu,Zhengan Yan,Yukun Xie,Kerui Shen,Jinkui Ren,Yuqi Lin,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinCa是一个基于可学习 invertible networks 的特征缓存框架，通过将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性属性的子组件，并应用差异化预测顺序来实现高效的特征预测，在仅增加不到0.2%参数的情况下实现了5-7倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像和视频生成中取得了显著成功，但迭代采样的高计算成本仍是实际部署的关键瓶颈。现有训练-free方法采用统一预测策略，无法适应异构特征动态，在高加速比下导致显著的质量下降。

Method: 提出LinCa框架，包含三个步骤：1) Decompose（分解）：通过轻量级可逆网络将缓存特征分解为具有不同连续性属性的子组件；2) Predict（预测）：为不同模型和 timestep 段训练独立的预测器，应用差异化预测顺序；3) Reconstruct（重建）：利用可逆网络的严格可逆性实现无损重建回原始特征空间。

Result: 在FLUX、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo上的实验表明，LinCa仅增加不到0.2%的额外参数，显著优于现有方法，并在5-7倍加速下保持接近无损的质量。

Conclusion: LinCa通过可逆网络实现特征分解与差异化预测，有效解决了异构特征动态的适应问题，在保持高质量的同时实现了显著加速，为扩散模型的实用化部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image and video generation, yet the high computational cost of iterative sampling remains a critical bottleneck for practical deployment. Feature caching has emerged as a promising acceleration paradigm by reusing or predicting intermediate features across timesteps. However, existing training-free methods apply uniform prediction strategies that cannot adapt to the heterogeneous feature dynamics, causing significant quality degradation under high acceleration ratios. We propose LinCa, a feature caching framework based on learnable invertible networks. LinCa decomposes cached features into sub-components with distinct continuity properties via a lightweight invertible network and applies differentiated prediction orders matched to each component. The strict invertibility guarantees lossless reconstruction back to the original feature space, forming a unified Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct pipeline. By training separate predictors for different models and timestep segments, LinCa adapts to heterogeneous feature dynamics. Experiments on FLUX, Qwen-Image, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LinCa, with less than 0.2% additional parameters, significantly outperforms existing methods and maintains near-lossless quality at 5-7x speedup. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/LinCa

</details>


### [80] [Dual Co-Train: Cross-Dataset Ultrasound Tongue Segmentation Under Extreme Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17983)
*Alisher Myrgyyassov,Zhen Song,Bruce Xiao Wang,Yu Sun,Min Ney Wong,Yihao Zhou,Yongping Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需源数据的域适应框架，用于超声舌像分割。该方法从仅使用5张标注源图像预训练的模型开始，通过迭代细化伪标签、使用基于轮廓的质量控制模块过滤不可靠掩码，以及通过分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格的合成图像-掩码对来实现域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声舌轮廓分割在跨数据集域偏移下仍具有挑战性，有限的标注、探头变异和采集噪声经常降低模型泛化能力。当前方法在域适应场景下面临源数据不可访问的约束。

Method: 提出基于UltraUNet骨干网络的源-free域适应框架。核心组件包括：(1)迭代伪标签细化；(2)基于轮廓的质量控制模块过滤不可靠掩码；(3)分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成样本；(4)学生模型在干净伪标签、噪声伪标签（带一致性正则化）和合成样本的混合上训练，实现闭环适应。

Result: 在12个跨8个超声舌成像数据集的源-目标迁移对上进行评估，并进行源规模缩放实验和消融研究。在所有比较中，该框架在分割重叠和轮廓精度上均优于基线方法，包括有监督方法。

Conclusion: 任务特定的伪标签精化和合成目标风格增强可显著改善超声舌成像的源-free域适应性能，为医学影像域适应提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Ultrasound tongue contour segmentation remains challenging under cross-dataset domain shift, where limited annotations, probe variability, and acquisition noise often degrade model generalization. We present a source-free domain adaptation framework for robust ultrasound tongue segmentation built on a lightweight UltraUNet backbone. Starting from a checkpoint pretrained on only five labeled source images, simulating an underfitted constrained source model, the proposed method adapts to a fully-unlabeled target domain by iteratively refining pseudo-labels, filtering unreliable masks with a contour-based quality-control module, and generating target-style synthetic image-mask pairs through a segmentation-guided conditional GAN. The student model is then trained on a mixture of clean pseudo-labeled target images, noisy pseudo-labels with consistency regularization, and synthetic samples, enabling closed-loop adaptation without access to source data. We evaluate the method on 12 source-target transfer pairs across eight ultrasound tongue imaging datasets, and conduct source-size scaling experiments and ablation studies. Across all comparisons, the proposed framework improves segmentation overlap and contour accuracy over the baselines, including supervised ones. These results suggest that task-specific pseudo-label refinement and synthetic target-style augmentation can substantially improve source-free adaptation for ultrasound tongue imaging.

</details>


### [81] [From Corpora to Co-Evolving Capabilities: Capability-Centric Data Design for Generalist Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18076)
*Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Taihang Hu,Jun Zheng,Qing Jin,Qinye Zhou,Zhengtao Wu,Yongchao Du,Zuan Gao,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Yuhang Yu,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个能力驱动的数据基础设施，将特定任务的监督构建与能力对齐的课程调度相结合，用于大规模图像生成。该系统包含三个专业化的数据引擎，构建文本-图像对齐、图像间转换和图像-知识关联的监督，并通过多阶段课程表联合优化任务组成、视觉概念分布、数据质量和图像分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流程通常孤立优化特定任务的数据集，面临如何组织异构监督以匹配生成能力依赖关系的核心挑战。

Method: 1)三个专业且可互操作的数据引擎分别处理文本-图像对齐、图像间转换和图像-知识关联；2)_caption experts对齐T2I和编辑监督；3)多阶段课程表沿能力获取依赖顺序联合演化任务组成、视觉概念分布、数据质量和图像分辨率；4)能力感知评估通过目标检索、专家构建和差距感知重采样闭环。

Result: 构建了440M图像T2I语料库、120M编辑对和超过27M图像-实体对；训练了3B和6B规模的多模态扩散模型；在CPI-Bench上进行了定量评估，并在多种文本到图像和编辑场景中进行了定性评估，展现了广泛的视觉覆盖、多功能渲染和跨生成能力的有效迁移。

Conclusion: 该框架通过能力驱动的数据基础设施和课程调度策略，能够有效组织大规模异构监督数据，提升多模态扩散模型在文本到图像生成和编辑任务上的综合性能。

Abstract: Large-scale image generation has benefited from advances in data scale, quality, rebalancing, and recaptioning, yet conventional pipelines typically optimize task-specific datasets in isolation. A central challenge is not only how to curate each task-specific corpus, but also how to organize heterogeneous supervision according to the dependencies among generative capabilities. We present a \textbf{{capability-driven data infrastructure}} that couples capability-specific supervision construction with capability-aligned curriculum scheduling. Its three specialized yet interoperable data engines build complementary relational supervision for text-image grounding, inter-image transformation, and image-knowledge association, while caption experts align T2I and editing supervision across tasks and granularities. A multi-stage curriculum jointly evolves task composition, visual-concept distribution, data quality, and image resolution along the dependency order of capability acquisition, with capability-aware evaluation closing the loop through targeted retrieval, expert construction, and gap-aware resampling. At scale, the framework curates a 440M-image T2I corpus, 120M editing pairs, and over 27M image-entity pairs. With this infrastructure, we train multimodal diffusion models at two scales from scratch, with 3B and 6B sizes respectively. We conduct quantitative evaluation on CPI-Bench, along with qualitative evaluations across diverse text-to-image and editing scenarios. Experimental results present broad visual coverage, versatile rendering, and effective transfer across generative capabilities.

</details>


### [82] [GS-Voxel: Fitting-Free Structured Latents for Large-Scale 3DGS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17988)
*Ming Qian,Zijian Wang,Minchao Sun,Jincheng Xiong,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Chi Wang,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-Voxel是一个无需拟合的_structured latent框架，用于将预优化的3D Gaussian Splatting(3DGS)重建转换为稀疏活跃体素，并利用factorized VAE分别编码体素几何和局部高斯属性，实现基于图像条件流模型的大规模航空3DGS场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的潜在3D生成器通常在结构化张量上操作，而预优化的3DGS重建是无序的、空间不规则的，且基元数量差异很大。现有方法缺乏一种有效的将无序3DGS转换为结构化潜在表示的方案。

Method: 1)将兼容的预优化3DGS重建确定性转换为稀疏活跃体素，保留所选基元的子体素位置和渲染属性；2)使用GS-specific factorized VAE分别编码体素几何和局部高斯属性为稀疏3D潜在向量；3)在GS-Voxel潜在空间中训练图像条件流模型；4)采用重叠感知的平铺推理扩展合成范围。

Result: GS-Voxel为预优化航空3DGS重建提供了结构化潜在表示，潜在容量随占用体素数量增长，而非受限于固定的场景级基元计数。

Conclusion: GS-Voxel成功实现了大规模航空3DGS场景的生成，无需额外per-scene优化，潜在容量可扩展，为航空遥感场景的生成式建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Many scalable latent 3D generators operate on structured tensors, whereas pre-optimized 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions are unordered, spatially irregular, and vary widely in primitive count. We present GS-Voxel, a fitting-free structured latent framework, and evaluate it for large-scale aerial 3D Gaussian scene generation. GS-Voxel deterministically converts a compatible pre-optimized 3DGS reconstruction into sparse active voxels without additional per-scene optimization, retaining the sub-voxel positions and rendering attributes of the selected primitives. A GS-specific factorized VAE then separately encodes voxel geometry and local Gaussian attributes into sparse 3D latents whose size grows with the number of occupied voxels rather than being limited by a fixed scene-wide primitive count. We train image-conditioned flow models in the GS-Voxel latent space to generate aerial 3DGS scenes. A key application enabled by GS-Voxel is large-area scene generation: overlap-aware tiled inference extends synthesis beyond a single training crop conditioned on satellite-view images. Our results show that GS-Voxel provides structured latents for pre-optimized aerial 3DGS reconstructions, with latent capacity that grows with the number of occupied voxels.

</details>


### [83] [AViTS: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Token Selection for Efficient Dynamic-Resolution Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17995)
*Haoran Qin,Zhengan Yan,Shikang Zheng,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Yuqi Lin,Chang Zou,JinShan Liu,Peiliang Cai,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AViTS是一种用于动态分辨率Diffusion Transformers的自适应时空token选择框架，通过latent-text注意力建模空间重要性和token级特征变化建模时间重要性，实现对关键token的选择性上采样，从而在保持生成质量的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)虽然能实现高质量生成，但因迭代采样而计算成本高昂。现有动态分辨率采样在分辨率转换时统一上采样所有latent tokens，导致冗余计算且可能破坏细粒度细节一致性。现有部分上采样策略难以同时捕捉token-text语义相关性和扩散步骤间的token表示动态。

Method: AViTS提出自适应时空token选择框架，包含：1) 通过latent-text注意力建模空间重要性；2) 通过跨扩散时间步的token级特征变化建模时间重要性；3) 融合空时重要性以实现选择性上采样，优先对关键token进行分辨率细化，延迟处理次要token。

Result: AViTS在FLUX上实现最高6.34x加速，在Qwen-Image-Edit和FLUX.1-Kontext-dev上实现近9x FLOPs reduction。与蒸馏模型结合可达14.76x加速。该方法与蒸馏、量化、特征缓存正交。

Conclusion: AViTS通过自适应时空token选择有效降低了动态分辨率DiTs的计算冗余，在显著提升效率的同时保持了生成质量，为扩散模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve high-quality generation but are costly due to iterative sampling. Dynamic-resolution sampling reduces early-stage cost by denoising at low resolution; however, uniformly upsampling all latent tokens at resolution transitions incurs redundant computation and may degrade fine-detail consistency. Existing partial upsampling strategies typically rely on local latent structure cues or single-step statistics, making it difficult to jointly capture token-text semantic relevance and token-wise representation dynamics across diffusion steps. We propose AViTS, an adaptive spatiotemporal token selection framework for dynamic-resolution DiTs. AViTS models spatial importance via latent-text attention and temporal importance via token-level feature variation across diffusion timesteps, and fuses them to enable spatiotemporal importance-aware selective upsampling: it prioritizes resolution refinement for critical tokens while deferring less important ones, thereby reducing redundant high-resolution computation and improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. AViTS achieves up to 6.34x on FLUX and nearly 9x FLOPs reduction on Qwen-Image-Edit and FLUX.1-Kontext-dev, orthogonal to distillation, quantization, and feature caching, and reaching 14.76x with distilled models. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/AViTS

</details>


### [84] [Memory Tree Guided Key Frame Querying for Efficient 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18009)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Fu-Chen Chen,Li-Wu Tsao,Cheng-Han Lee,Che-Chun Su,Lu Xia,Ronghui Peng,Jenq-Neng Hwang,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Answering questions accurately and efficiently in embodied scenarios presents significant challenges due to limited computational and memory resources for Vision Language Model (VLM) inference. Existing methods adopt visual search key frame retrieval method to select critical question-related key frames for VLM input. However, visual search methods are inefficient because they require visual search among thousands of video frames for each individual user query. In this work, we propose a memory tree guided key frame selection paradigm for efficient 3D question answering in embodied scenarios. Our method leverages a compact and reusable 3D scene representation, termed MemTree3D, which supports real-time online construction leveraging camera 6-DoF poses. MemTree3D captures multi-level 3D scene information, enabling a Large Language Model to efficiently query and retrieve question-relevant key frames through our scoring-based frame selection without reprocessing the entire video stream. On OpenEQA, our method improves the LLM-Match of GPT-4o by 17.4%, LLaVA-OneVision-7B by 5.8%, outperforms existing visual search methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/MemTree3D

</details>


### [85] [Automated ACL Footprint Identification Using 3D Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18012)
*Ruida Cheng,Ali Uneri,Gabriel Gibson,Frances T. Sheehan,Barry Boden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究开发了两种3D深度学习模型用于从3D MR图像中直接识别ACL股骨足迹中心，基于图像的方法以2.1mm平均误差优于基于模型的2.8mm，为ACL重建手术提供更精确的临床解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前交叉韧带(ACL)重建失败的主要原因之一是股骨隧道定位错误，这可能导致半月板病变和骨关节炎的发生。准确识别ACL股骨足迹对于精确放置隧道、恢复膝关节原生力学特性、术后膝关节健康及预防移植物失败至关重要。然而，目前人工智能研究主要集中在ACL分割和破裂分类，而非ACL足迹中心的识别。

Method: 开发了两种综合3D深度学习架构：1) 基于3D图卷积神经网络的geometric model，应用到3D股骨网格；2) 基于3D MR图像的3D landmark-enhanced identification model。使用来自公开数据库的4883个右膝和3087个左膝图像集，80%用于模型训练，20%用于测试。

Result: 两种模型均取得优异性能，但基于图像的方法优于基于模型的方法(平均误差2.1mm vs 2.8mm)。

Conclusion: 3D深度学习为ACL足迹定位提供了一种可行的临床方法，有潜力提高ACL重建足印定位的准确性。

Abstract: One of the most common reasons for anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) reconstruction failure is femoral tunnel malpositioning (ACL footprint center and tunnel orientation). Such failures may lead to the development of meniscal pathology and osteoarthritis. Accurate ACL femoral footprint identification is therefore essential for precise tunnel placement, restoration of the native knee joint mechanics, post-surgical knee joint health and prevention of graft failure. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) bring new opportunities to improve image-guided orthopedic surgery. However, at present, existing AI research focuses primarily on ACL segmentation and rupture classification based on pre- and post-operative magnetic resonance (MR) images. Identification of the ACL footprint center using deep learning methods has not been thoroughly researched. Thus, the purpose of this study is to explore 3D deep learning models for ACL femoral footprint identification directly from 3D MR images. Two comprehensive 3D deep learning architectures were developed: a 3D graph convolutional neural network-based geometric model applied to 3D femoral meshes; and a 3D landmark-enhanced identification model based on 3D MR images. A total of 4883 right and 3087 left knee image sets were used from a publicly available database. Eighty percent (80%) were applied to model generation, and twenty percent (20%) were preserved for model testing. Both models achieved excellent performance; however, the image-based method outperformed the model-based method (average error of 2.1mm vs 2.8 mm). Thus, 3D deep learning provides a feasible clinical approach for ACL footprint localization and has the potential to improve ACL reconstruction footprint accuracy.

</details>


### [86] [Initialization-Free Bundle Adjustment Revisited: A Controlled Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18028)
*Simon Weber,Mateo de Mayo,Je Hyeong Hong,Carl Olsson,Daniel Cremers,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究初始化无关的束束调整(InitFree BA)，旨在直接从图像观测中恢复相机位姿和场景结构，避免传统运动恢复结构的几何初始化阶段。研究发现存在"优化-重建差距"：低目标函数值并不一定能产生有效的度量3D重建，关键挑战在于获得可可靠度量升级的投影重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有InitFree BA方法基于对象空间误差(OSE)和变量投影(VarPro)公式，从随机相机配置开始优化表现出良好行为。但现有评估主要关注优化成功与否，不清楚低OSE是否真的能产生有效的度量3D重建。该问题在计算机视觉社区基本未被探索。

Method: 提出统一评估框架：1) C++实现的OSE公式；2) Blender数据集生成器，提供精确真值和受控的相机配置与观测密度。通过该框架进行系统实验分析。

Result: 发现先前被忽视的优化-重建差距：具有相似低OSE值的投影解在度量升级后可能导致截然不同的欧氏重建。识别出三个关键因素：初始化先验、地标观测密度和度量升级稳定性。

Conclusion: InitFree BA的主要挑战不仅是最小化OSE目标函数，更是获得能够进行可靠度量升级的投影重建。提出的基准、实现和分析为未来初始化无关束调整研究建立了更强的实验基础。

Abstract: Initialization-free bundle adjustment (InitFree BA) aims to recover camera poses and scene structure directly from image observations, avoiding the geometric initialization stages of conventional structure-from-motion pipelines. Recent methods based on Object-Space Error (OSE) formulations and Variable Projection (VarPro) show encouraging optimization behavior from random camera configurations. However, existing evaluations primarily measure optimization success, leaving unclear whether a low OSE objective yields a valid metric 3D reconstruction. We revisit InitFree BA experimentally through a unified evaluation framework combining a C++ implementation of existing OSE formulations with a Blender-based dataset generator providing exact ground truth and controlled camera configurations and observation densities. Our experiments reveal a previously overlooked optimization--reconstruction gap: projective solutions with similarly low OSE values can lead to substantially different Euclidean reconstructions after metric upgrade. We identify initialization priors, landmark observation density, and metric-upgrade stability as key factors governing reconstruction success. Overall, our results suggest that the main challenge of InitFree BA is not merely minimizing OSE objectives, but obtaining projective reconstructions that admit reliable metric upgrade. We believe that the proposed benchmark, implementation, and analysis establish stronger experimental foundations for future research on initialization-free bundle adjustment, a problem largely unexplored within the computer vision community. Project page is available at https://github.com/simonwebertum/InitFreeBA.git.

</details>


### [87] [Deep Academic Survey: Stateful Agentic Closed-Loop Paradigm for Academic Survey Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18034)
*Zhikai Xu,Zhucun Xue,Teng Hu,Yabiao Wang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAS是一个有状态代理框架,用于生成面向出版的学术综述。它基于包含约200万篇论文调查导向表示的DAS-2M动态元数据湖,通过维护文献、组织、写作和最终化状态来协调论文理解、知识组织、证据支撑的起草和手稿验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究和自动综述生成系统通常不能协调论文理解、文献组织、证据支撑的起草和手稿验证,需要一个共享的、可修订的状态来协调这些环节。

Method: 关键思路是将可重用的论文分析与特定主题的手稿构建分离。代理通过基于候选证据的分类学规划、逆向论文到章节路由和分层声明及引用规划来维护显式的文献、组织、写作和最终化状态。语义审查仅重新激活受影响的写作状态进行修复和重新评估,形成具有确定性验证的 scoped 闭环。

Result: 在DAS-Bench(30个主题基准)和DAS-Eval(16个评估标准)上评估,DAS在所有四个维度(学术引用质量、分类学综合、分层话语和手稿组装可靠性)均获得最高平均分,总体得分4.34,最强竞争对手为4.03。盲法专家评估进一步显示DAS在30个主题中27个优于Naive RAG,在21个共享CS主题中19个优于AutoSurvey。

Conclusion: DAS是一个创新的有状态代理框架,成功解决了学术综述生成的协调问题,通过分离论文分析与手稿构建、使用显式状态管理和scoped闭环验证,在多个评估维度上显著优于现有最佳系统。

Abstract: Academic surveys play a central role in organizing rapidly expanding scholarly literature, yet their construction requires extensive paper analysis, coherent knowledge organization, fine-grained citation support, and reliable manuscript assembly. Existing Deep Research and automated survey generation systems address parts of this process, but typically do not coordinate paper understanding, literature organization, evidence-grounded drafting, and manuscript validation through a shared, revisable state. We introduce DAS, a stateful agentic framework for generating publication-oriented academic surveys. Its key idea is to separate reusable paper analysis from topic-specific manuscript construction. DAS builds on DAS-2M, a dynamically updated metadata lake containing survey-oriented representations of approximately two million papers. Its agents maintain explicit literature, organization, writing, and finalization states through candidate-grounded taxonomy planning, reverse paper-to-section routing, and hierarchical claim and citation planning. Semantic review reactivates only the affected writing states for repair and reevaluation, forming a scoped closed loop with deterministic validation. We further introduce DAS-Bench, a 30-topic benchmark, together with DAS-Eval, which assesses scholarly citation quality, taxonomic synthesis, hierarchical discourse, and manuscript assembly reliability through 16 criteria. Among systems evaluated on all 30 topics, DAS achieves the highest average in all four dimensions, with an overall score of 4.34 compared with 4.03 for the strongest competitor, and the same ordering is preserved on the matched 21-topic CS subset. Blinded expert evaluation further prefers DAS to Naive RAG on 27 of 30 topics and to AutoSurvey on 19 of 21 shared CS topics. The project page is available at https://zhikaixu24.github.io/projects/DAS/.

</details>


### [88] [Plug-and-Play Traffic Element Awareness for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18035)
*Zongzheng Zhang,Jijun Wang,Saining Zhang,Shuo Wang,Yiru Wang,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Yuwen Heng,Hao Sun,Anqing Jiang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次系统研究交通元素（红绿灯、路标）在端到端自动驾驶中的影响，通过为多个公开数据集添加交通元素标注，并设计通用集成框架，在多种架构和数据集上验证了交通元素感知能够持续提升驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶研究主要关注动态道路参与者（车辆、行人），而交通元素对人类驾驶决策至关重要却未被充分探索。公开数据集缺乏结构化交通元素标注，且现代驾驶系统架构多样，导致该领域缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 构建统一研究基础设施，为多个公开驾驶数据集补充全面交通元素标注；采用最小化通用集成设计，以即插即用方式将交通元素信号融入现有管道，架构修改极少；跨多种范式评估：感知-预测-规划流程、视觉语言动作模型(VLA)、回归规划器、扩散策略和轨迹评分框架。

Result: 在nuScenes、NAVSIM-v1、NAVSIM-v2和Bench2Drive上，所有范式和数据集均显示性能持续提升；在挑战性的NAVSIM-v2基准上显著提升最先进架构，建立新SOTA。

Conclusion: 交通元素感知为端到端驾驶系统提供稳健且可泛化的信号，这种简单集成方法在多样架构中具有普适性价值。

Abstract: Traffic elements such as traffic lights and road signs play a fundamental role in human driving decisions and should naturally influence end-to-end driving performance. However, existing end-to-end driving research predominantly focuses on dynamic road participants (e.g., vehicles and pedestrians), while the role of traffic elements remains largely unexplored. The community still lacks a systematic study quantifying their impact, largely because public datasets rarely provide structured traffic-element annotations and modern driving systems vary widely in architecture and training paradigm. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of traffic element awareness for end-to-end autonomous driving. We construct a unified research infrastructure by augmenting multiple public driving datasets with comprehensive traffic-element annotations. To support diverse model families, we adopt a minimal and universal integration design that incorporates traffic-element signals into existing pipelines in a plug-and-play manner with negligible architectural modification. We evaluate this design across modern paradigms, including perception-prediction-planning pipelines, vision-language-action models (VLA), regression-based planners, diffusion-based policies, and trajectory-scoring frameworks, on nuScenes, NAVSIM-v1, NAVSIM-v2, and Bench2Drive. Across all paradigms and datasets, this simple integration consistently improves driving performance, demonstrating that traffic element awareness provides a robust and generalizable signal for end-to-end driving systems. Notably, on the challenging NAVSIM-v2 benchmark, our approach significantly improves state-of-the-art architectures and data pipelines, establishing a new state of the art.

</details>


### [89] [EDITBRIDGE: Towards Faithful and Efficient Ultra-High-Resolution Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18063)
*Jiayi Song,Shijie Huang,Fangtai Wu,Yubo Huang,Zhenxiong Tan,Songhua Liu,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditBridge是一个用于高效超高清图像编辑的扩散桥框架，通过从低分辨率编辑结果到高分辨率的结构化数据到数据转换来实现4K分辨率图像编辑，并引入先验引导的块稀疏注意力机制来降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型受限于二次注意力复杂度和内存需求，难以处理1K以上分辨率。常用的两阶段pipeline（低分辨率编辑+独立超分辨率）存在信息分歧（细节幻觉与原图矛盾）和纹理退化（过度平滑或过度锐化）问题。

Method: 提出扩散桥框架将 refinement 表征为从低分辨率编辑结果到高分辨率结果的結構化数据到数据转换，明确以原始高分辨率图像为条件来保留真实细节。引入先验引导的块稀疏注意力机制，利用第一阶段编辑的语义对应将跨图像交互约束到空间对齐区域，显著降低计算开销。

Result: 在高达4K分辨率下实现高保真编辑，2K分辨率下达到3.6-8.4倍加速，4K编辑仅需61秒。

Conclusion: EditBridge通过扩散桥框架和先验引导的块稀疏注意力机制，有效解决了超高清图像编辑中的信息分歧和纹理退化问题，在保持细节真实性的同时实现了高效计算。

Abstract: High-resolution image editing is increasingly demanded in professional workflows, yet existing diffusion-based models remain constrained to resolutions below 1K due to quadratic attention complexity and prohibitive memory requirements. A prevalent workaround employs a two-stage pipeline: editing at low resolution followed by independent super-resolution. However, this approach suffers from two critical issues: information divergence, where hallucinated details contradict the original high-resolution (HR) source, and texture degradation, manifesting as over-smoothed or over-sharpened artifacts. We propose EditBridge, a diffusion bridge framework for efficient ultra high-resolution editing. Unlike conventional diffusion that regenerates from noise, we formulate refinement as structured data-to-data translation from the low-resolution (LR) edited result to its HR counterpart, explicitly conditioned on the original HR source to preserve authentic details. To efficiently incorporate HR source guidance, we introduce a prior-guided block-wise sparse attention mechanism that exploits semantic correspondence from first-stage editing to constrain cross-image interactions to spatially aligned regions, significantly reducing computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EditBridge achieves high-fidelity editing with superior perceptual quality at resolutions up to 4K, delivering 3.6--8.4$\times$ speedup at 2K and enabling practical 4K editing in 61 seconds.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GxP-Agent系统,通过将监管流程编码为有向无环图(DAG),将单一的数据集生成任务分解为15个领域特定节点执行,解决LLM在临床试验编程中失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验编程是将研究方案转换为符合CDISC标准的分析数据集,是监管提交中的瓶颈,但前沿LLM在此任务中完全失败(11次单次尝试均无法生成有效的主题级分析数据集)。

Method: 提出GxP-Agent多智能体系统:1)将监管流程排序编码为DAG拓扑;2)将整体数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点;3)由具备pharmaverse技能上下文的worker agents执行;4)设置验证门和条件重试机制。在FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01(254 subjects, 49个ground-truth ADSL变量)上构建CDISC-Bench执行基准进行评估。

Result: 使用Claude Sonnet 4.6的GxP-Agent在CDISC-Bench上实现100%结构匹配(49/49变量,254条正确记录),三次独立运行均成功。最佳检索增强基线为59.2%,所有单智能体和平面多智能体方法均为0%。DAG拓扑使较弱模型也能提升性能:GPT-4.1在相同DAG下达到59.2%平均结构匹配,而在其他架构下为0%。该方法推广到ADAE(不良事件;9节点分支DAG,55变量,1191条记录),首次尝试即达100%结构匹配。

Conclusion: 将领域流程知识编码为图拓扑而非仅依赖LLM推理,是实现可靠、GxP合规的临床试验编程的关键使能因素。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [91] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aegis是一个运行时治理系统,通过在模型输出和工具执行之间引入可信决策层,将模型输出视为操作提议,在执行前进行审核评估,以防止AI代理系统产生有害的操作副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理系统(Agentic AI)能够执行修改文件、发送消息、启动作业等操作,这将安全问题从有害文本生成转变为有害操作副作用。现有提示级治理无法创建执行边界,需要运行时治理来阻止风险操作。

Method: Aegis采用"模型提议-可信运行时决定"的架构:1)将模型输出作为行动提案;2)根据活动策略状态评估提案;3)服务端解决溯源;4)不确定时关闭;5)通过Senate-style settlement(基于多数票的非单边授权路径)路由选定案例。

Result: 在6300行比较数据中,提示策略条件下产生79行风险比较器路径泄漏;在2100行Aegis治理数据中,系统记录零受治理的模拟工具应用和零受治理的风险副作用完成。所有1832行Aegis尝试保留可信的Aegis解决溯源,1019行Senate解决案例均有多数票和最终签名计票证据。

Conclusion: 结果表明在评估的沙盒语料库中,运行时动作边界治理成功阻止了观察到的风险提案成为受治理的副作用。但这些结果不能证明通用自主智能体的安全性。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [92] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在资源受限情况下，有界智能体如何在记忆（利用自身历史）和信号传递（同伴通信）之间分配信息预算的问题，提出"记忆-信号边界"概念来描述实现性能阈值所需的记忆和消息率配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在认知和通信资源有限的情况下，智能体需要决定是从自身历史中获取信息还是从同伴处获取信息。理解这种权衡对于优化协作任务的效率具有重要意义。

Method: 采用参照游戏（referential games）作为实验范式，通过操纵记忆和消息率来估计记忆-信号边界，并检验相关预测。

Result: 初步实验发现，目标重复与较短的成功消息相关，而隐藏循环规则的可预测性并未缩短消息长度。

Conclusion: 提出假设：当历史提供更大的损失减少时，有界智能体需要较少的同伴通信。实验可以跨协作任务检验这一预测。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [93] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究聚焦于AI API合同中的"推理努力"条款，通过对比Sonnet 5模型在明确高努力与省略该条款两种合同下的表现，发现高努力合同平均每调用成本增加$0.01031，但准确率差异不显著（+1.33%），导致每正确回答成本反而更高（$0.08665 vs $0.07662）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API买家购买的是包含模型、推理努力条款、输出限制、服务产品、提示和价格计划的完整合同，而非仅模型名称。研究者试图理解推理努力条款对模型性能和经济效率的实际影响。

Method: 使用30道AIME 2026数学竞赛题目，每题5次调用，对比Sonnet 5明确高努力合同与省略努力条款合同的差异。每轮付费尝试分配一个冻结的终端类别，通过推理重采样保留重复调用。

Result: 高努力合同每调用成本增加$0.01031 [+$0.00204, +$0.01974]；准确率对比为+0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]，统计上不显著。每正确回答成本：高努力合同$0.08662，省略合同$0.07662。跨提供商的分析显示模型特定的省略语义存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究未检测到推理努力条款带来的准确率显著提升，但成本确实增加。该设计无法排除高努力合同最多带来4.67个百分点准确率增益的可能性。结果限于所研究模型、任务和收集日期。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [94] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KernelArc是一个多智能体框架，用于跨异构工作负载的自主GPU内核优化，通过策略专业化智能体并行运行和协调，在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上实现了先进的性能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的核心动机是：共享多智能体搜索可以扩大探索范围，在固定候选预算内达到更强的当前最优解，同时个体协调特征的价值取决于内核和优化阶段。

Method: 策略专业化智能体并行运行，通过结论-only共享内存、确定性基准guard、只读跨智能体状态和plateau-triggered drafting进行协调。在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上使用SOL-ExecBench工作负载进行评估。

Result: 实现了多种自定义内核优化，包括BF16 GEMM、静态cuBLASLt Expert-API配置表、融合的MoE反向传播、shape-gated解码器层融合、native NVFP4分组查询注意力和分页预填充注意力。在2026年7月30日的SOL-ExecBench公开排行榜上，在代表性的L1、Quantization和FlashInfer任务中排名第一。

Conclusion: 共享多智能体搜索能够扩大探索范围并在固定预算内达到更强的性能，而个体协调特征的价值依赖于具体内核和优化阶段。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{{}} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [95] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedPref是一种联邦学习方法,用于从放射学报告中提取结构化信息(JSON格式)。通过让各机构协作训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器,实现在不共享原始报告或注释的情况下,利用分布不均的数据进行联合学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告以自由文本形式描述发现和位置,但下游搜索和分析需要固定模式的关系。提取模型的学习需要标注数据,而这些数据在不同机构间分布不均:小医院本地证据较少,数据池化可能不可行(隐私问题)。因此需要一种联邦学习方法,使拥有不平等、未池化数据的机构能够从协作中受益。

Method: FedPref方法:(1)冻结的公共语言模型提出替代JSON提取方案;(2)本地注释对其进行排序;(3)各站点协作训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器,仅共享模型更新;(4)异构教师池在重复单模型样本崩溃时提供跨模型对比。

Result: 在六个模拟医院的开发数据上(数据量和疾病患病率不均),FedPref相比各站点独立训练:客户端平均F1提升2.49点,最差站点F1提升9.10点,最大收益在数据最少的站点。中心化训练在池化偏好对并集上比客户端平均F1高2.66点。在400份报告的手动验证金标准测试集上,FedPref达到68.68 F1,池化训练达到71.67 F1,排名顺序一致。

Conclusion: FedPref使拥有不平等、未池化数据的机构能够从协作中受益,而无需共享报告或注释。该方法在数据最少的站点获得最大收益,有效解决了医疗数据孤岛和隐私保护之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [96] [Task-Aware Harness Provisioning for LLM Agents in Mission-Critical Infrastructure Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17433)
*Liangtao Lin,Qingang Zhang,Zhaomeng Zhu,Tianwei Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何为运行关键基础设施(MCI)的大语言模型(LLM)智能体优化配置harness（信息访问、工具使用和操作权限的访问控制机制）。现有系统对所有任务统一配置完整harness，造成资源浪费。本文将harness配置问题转化为资源匹配问题，通过数学方法对MCI任务进行分类，对harness配置进行排序，并从文献挖掘和受控智能体执行两个来源构建任务到harness的映射。提出map-guided escalation算法，从任务特定harness开始，仅在自检失败后扩展到完整配置。在液冷场景中，智能体准确率从0.652提升到0.715，且与Reflexion相比节省48%token；电力场景中完整配置仍最优，但基于映射的配置提供更低成本方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行关键基础设施(MCI)的LLM智能体通常依赖harness来控制信息访问、工具使用和操作权限。现有系统对所有任务统一提供完整的harness配置，但这可能造成不必要的资源浪费，因为不同任务对harness的需求不同。

Method: 1.将harness配置问题建模为资源匹配问题；2.基于底层系统的数学表示对MCI任务进行分类；3.对harness配置按信息量和类型进行排序；4.从两个来源构建任务到harness的映射：研究文献挖掘和受控智能体执行测量；5.提出map-guided escalation算法：从任务特定harness开始，仅在自检失败后扩展到完整配置。

Result: 液冷场景：智能体准确率从0.652(full provision)提升到0.715，节省48%token；电力场景：full provision仍最优，但基于映射的配置提供更低成本替代方案。

Conclusion: harness配置遵循领域依赖的准确率-成本Pareto前沿，而非通用最优解。不同MCI领域需要不同的harness配置策略。

Abstract: LLM agents have been widely adopted to operate mission-critical infrastructure (MCI). These agents normally rely on a harness that determines what information they can access, which tools they can use, and what actions they can take. Existing systems often expose the same comprehensive harness to every task, which may not be necessary and cause resource wastes. In this paper, we focus on the identification of optimal harness configurations, and view it as a resource-matching problem between what each task requires and what the harness provides. To measure this match, we classify MCI tasks based on the mathematical representation of the underlying system and rank harness configurations by the amount and type of information they provide. We then construct task-to-harness mappings from two sources: mining research literature and measuring controlled agent execution. Leveraging the measured mapping, we propose a new harness provisioning algorithm: map-guided escalation. It begins with a task-specific harness and expands to full provision only after a failed self-check. We evaluate our method in two representative MCI tasks: in liquid cooling, it improves the agent accuracy from 0.652 under full provision to 0.715 and achieves accuracy comparable to Reflexion with 48% fewer tokens; In power grids, full provision remains accuracy-optimal, while map-based provisioning offers lower-cost alternatives. These findings show that harness provisioning follows a domain-dependent accuracy-cost Pareto frontier rather than a universal optimum.

</details>


### [97] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FAR系统，将人类输入从预先选择的单个问题转变为感兴趣的研究方向，通过文献搜索、过滤和自动分诊级联流程，在组合数学领域成功识别了多个关于著名猜想的数学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI数学工作流程中，人类努力集中在开头（选择研究问题）和结尾（审查结果），这两个阶段正成为研究级数学的瓶颈。前沿模型推理是有限资源，专家数学审查更加稀缺，因此需要更好地分配这些稀缺资源。

Method: 构建了FAR系统，采用文献到评审的级联流程：1)从5,245篇组合数学论文中搜索候选猜想或开放问题；2)过滤保留看起来合理且仍未解决的猜想；3)进行推理和自动分诊；4)人工审核最终候选结果。

Result: 从5,245篇组合数学论文中提取了6,453个候选猜想或开放问题，过滤至4,717个看似表述良好且仍开放的猜想。推理和自动分诊阶段筛选出598个潜在解决方案，最终选出77项供作者团队审核，发现了多个有价值的数学发现。

Conclusion: 研究证明了这种人机协作新模式的有效性，在组合数学领域成功识别了多个有价值的数学发现，包括关于Davies-Jenssen-Perkins-Roberts、Erdős-Straus、Ikenmeyer-Pak-Panova和Lund-Saraf-Wolf等猜想和问题的结果。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [98] [AutoResearch: Insight In, Hallucination Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17906)
*Yiming Ren,Xiang Liu,Qumeng Sun,Xiao Zhang,Jiahao Li,Haoyang Zhang,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoResearch是一个两阶段自主研究系统，结合了创意生成和创意执行，旨在解决研究想法的形成及其通过实验可靠验证的问题。该系统在跨模态检索、系统优化和基准驱动的机器学习等代表性场景中，将生成的想法转化为可测量的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究系统虽然能够执行长时间的研究工作流程，但仅靠自动化并不能确保研究过程保持科学合理性。需要解决研究想法如何形成以及如何通过实验可靠验证的问题。

Method: 在创意生成阶段，系统持续整合新兴研究信号与累积的领域知识，识别可迁移的机制性洞察，使用多模型生成和交叉审查来产生有依据的、可测试的研究计划。在创意执行阶段，协调智能体将计划分解为实验，迭代实现和诊断，并在接受研究结论前采用独立的循证审查。

Result: 在RSICD基准上，AutoResearch生成的想法将平均召回率从32.84提升至34.69，同时仅记录5个审计确认的问题事件，而其他自主研究系统为11-27个。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在实验前使洞察有依据、在接受结论前使结论有依据的研究过程，实现了"洞察输入，幻觉输出"（Insight In, Hallucination Out）的目标。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are increasingly capable of executing long research workflows, yet automation alone does not ensure that the resulting process remains scientifically grounded. We introduce AutoResearch, a two-stage system that connects Idea Generation with Idea Execution to address both how research ideas are formed and how they are reliably established through experimentation. In Idea Generation, AutoResearch continuously integrates emerging research signals with accumulated domain knowledge, identifies transferable mechanistic insights, and uses multi-model generation and cross-review to produce grounded, testable research plans. In Idea Execution, coordinated agents decompose these plans into experiments, iteratively implement and diagnose them, and employ independent evidence-based review before accepting research conclusions. Across representative settings in cross-modal retrieval, systems optimization, and benchmark-driven machine learning, AutoResearch turns generated ideas into measurable progress, detects and corrects unreliable experimental results, and makes evidence-conditioned decisions to continue, revise, or terminate research directions. For example, on RSICD benchmark, an AutoResearch-generated idea improves mean Recall from 32.84 to 34.69, while recording only 5 audit-confirmed issue events compared with 11-27 for other autonomous research systems. These results demonstrate a research process in which meaningful insight is grounded before experimentation and conclusions are grounded before acceptance: Insight In, Hallucination Out.

</details>


### [99] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillEffect是一个检查 lowering 的运行时系统，解决语言模型将 Agent Skills 转换为代码时可能导致的内存溢出问题。该系统通过独立的检查器重建每个 lowering，配合关系插件和共享运行时机制来执行检查选择、有界 VM 执行、原子容量租赁和分阶段发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型将 Agent Skills 提供的程序化指导和资源义务转换为现有工具接口的代码时，即使语义正确的程序也可能加载整个输入并超出单次工具调用的可用内存。

Method: 提出 SkillEffect，一个具有可恢复源关系、审计的有界实现和注册输出后条件的检查 lowering 运行时。每个关系插件提供：源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界 IR 构造器、竞技场边界函数和后条件。通用运行时提供：检查选择、有界 VM 执行、原子容量租赁和分阶段发布。

Result: 跨 6 个操作符家族，有界访问显著降低峰值内存并在外部固定上限下提高完成率。6 个插件在 5 种执行模式中实例化相同契约。XLSX 入职研究和 Top-k 扩展显示新的关系和新的保留状态模式复用相同信任边界，检查器接受所有评估的合法配置并拒绝所有对抗性提议。

Conclusion: 一个检查 lowering 架构可以在 Agent 工具调度时强制执行异构注册内存关系。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [100] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiSCO是一种严格黑盒的零样本防御框架，通过分布引导的后缀扩展和对比评分优化，在不访问模型内部结构的情况下，有效降低text-to-image模型的NSFW内容生成风险，在I2P基准上实现高达37.7%的ASR降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着text-to-image生成模型的发展，其安全风险日益突出，特别是NSFW内容（如暴力和裸露）的生成问题。现有的白盒防御方法需要访问模型内部结构，无法扩展到专有模型；而现有的黑盒方法基于LLM重写prompt，但无法解决"良性对抗"问题——即那些语言上安全但由于模型学习的数据分布仍会导致有害生成的prompt。

Method: DiSCO采用分布引导的后缀扩展方法，通过beam search生成候选prompt，并利用目标模型自身生成的safe和unsafe图像池进行对比评分优化。该方法包含迭代自适应反馈机制，不断调整直到生成安全内容。整个过程完全在prompt层面操作，无需模型重训练、微调或访问模型内部结构。

Result: 在I2P基准测试和多种red-teaming攻击下，DiSCO显著提升了模型安全性：未防御模型上实现37.7%的ASR降低，防御模型上实现25.13%的ASR降低，同时保持了语义保真度并改善了图像一致性。

Conclusion: DiSCO作为首个严格黑盒的零样本防御框架，为text-to-image模型安全提供了新的解决思路。该方法不依赖模型内部信息，可应用于任何黑盒系统，具有很强的通用性和实用性。为text-to-image模型的安全防护提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{{benign adversarial}} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [101] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CASE方法，通过在答案token隐藏状态上训练线性门来选择最高分候选答案，解决大语言模型在困难问题上采样答案存在相关错误导致多数投票失效的问题，并引入decodability指标预测何时隐藏态选择优于投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在困难问题上不可靠，因为采样答案存在相关错误，错误答案可能获胜，更多样本反而使决策变差。现有基于隐藏态的正确性信号选择方法准确率不稳定，且缺乏可信赖度指标。

Method: 训练线性门(gate)在答案token隐藏状态上，选择得分最高的候选答案；提出decodability指标衡量模型隐藏态区分正确与错误候选答案的能力，该指标无泄露且可预测选择方法相对投票的准确率提升。

Result: 在通用和医学LLM上，CASE相比投票在中等难度问题上提升最多19分，困难问题上提升16.8分。Decodability与选择增益呈Pearson相关r=0.75，决策阈值约AUC=0.60。Decodability依赖于模型需回忆的对齐知识而非模型规模，且可迁移到未见过的科学领域（3.8分内）。

Conclusion: Decodability提供了实用的先验判据，可在给定模型和任务前衡量隐藏态选择与多数投票的优劣，从而指导融合方法选择。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [102] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 个人AI系统的观察能力受限于用户信任与合作。文章提出"合作观察"框架：系统构建用户生活模型，用户评估系统行为并控制观察范围，有用的行为可扩展观察渠道，失败则导致收缩。通过Organizm原型6个月单被试测试验证该框架的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI需要理解用户目标、约束和承诺来规划行动，但观察范围本身不能自动提升帮助质量。系统必须在信息选择和压缩中做出权衡，这一"观察瓶颈"具有合作性质。

Method: 提出"合作观察"理论框架，描述用户评估、系统行为和观察渠道扩展/收缩的反馈循环。并通过Organizm原型进行6个月单被试纵向测试。

Result: 初步单被试案例表明：有用且可检查的行为能使用户维持或扩展观察渠道；系统失败会导致用户纠正、缩小或撤销观察权限。

Conclusion: 合作观察可作为个人智能的设计框架，未来需进一步评估观察质量如何影响个人AI的性能表现。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [103] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KNOWSIM是一个评估框架，通过维护用户知识状态的显式表示（以信息单元图和先修关系的形式）来评估大型语言模型在知识密集型协作任务中的信息校准能力。该框架计算三个指标：知识增益、传递校准和认知过载，直接从知识状态轨迹中反映信息校准的关键机制方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于评估和训练LLM的用户模拟器没有显式建模用户知识状态，导致无法产生跨知识水平的真实交互，也无法反映交互如何随着知识演进而展开。这一差距需要一个新的评估框架来弥补。

Method: KNOWSIM框架采用基于学习理论的知识状态更新规则，将用户知识表示为具有先修关系的信息单元图。框架计算三个关键指标：Knowledge Gain（知识增益）、Delivery Calibration（传递校准）和Cognitive Overload（认知过载）。

Result: 在两个领域、705个人机会话（按知识水平分层）中进行验证，KNOWSIM的排名与人类判断显著一致（73-74%的符号一致率），优于三个基线模拟器。对9个LLM的测试揭示了最佳模型会因用户知识水平而变化，揭示了标准评估中不可见的 aptitude-treatment 交互作用。

Conclusion: KNOWSIM成功实现了对LLM信息校准能力的细粒度评估，通过显式建模用户知识状态的演进，为评估和训练更符合用户认知能力的对话式AI系统提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity. Yet user simulators used to evaluate and train LLMs do not explicitly model user knowledge so they neither produce realistic interactions across knowledge levels nor reflect how interactions unfold as that knowledge evolves. To close this gap, we introduce KNOWSIM, an evaluation framework built around a user simulator that maintains explicit knowledge states, represented as a graph of Information Units with prerequisite relationships, that evolve under update rules grounded in learning theory. KNOWSIM computes three metrics (Knowledge Gain, Delivery Calibration, Cognitive Overload) directly from the knowledge state trajectory, reflecting key mechanistic aspects of information calibration. We validate KNOWSIM against 705 human-AI sessions across two domains, stratified by knowledge level: its rankings align significantly with human judgments (73-74% sign agreement), outperforming three baseline simulators. Applied to 9 LLMs, KNOWSIM reveals that the best model shifts by user knowledge level, revealing aptitude-treatment interactions invisible to standard evaluation.

</details>


### [104] [Synthesizing Feature Extractors: An Agentic Approach for Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17170)
*Hai Xia,Carlos Ansótegui,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的自动化特征提取器合成方法，用于约束满足问题的算法选择。该方法通过agentic check-fix-verify循环生成可执行的Python脚本作为问题特定的特征提取器，在三个组合问题上测试结果显示其性能优于专家设计的mzn2feat特征和基于Transformer的trans2feat特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动设计特征提取器需要深厚的领域专业知识，且在新问题类别出现时会成为瓶颈。现有特征提取方法依赖于人工设计，效率低下且难以扩展。

Method: 使用LLM代理在check-fix-verify循环中自动合成可执行的Python脚本作为特征提取器。给定MiniZinc模型和实例，LLM代理生成代码构建类型化图表示并计算图密度、变量聚类、约束紧度等结构属性。

Result: 在车辆路径规划、汽车序列化和定长纠错码(FLECC)三个组合问题上，使用5个先进求解器进行评估。合成提取器生成的算法选择器始终优于专家设计的mzn2feat特征(在FLECC上最高提升8.3个百分点)和基于Transformer的trans2feat变体。

Conclusion: 该方法能够自动生成可解释的、特定问题的特征提取器，在算法选择任务上显著优于现有方法，同时保持特征提取器的可检查性和可解释性。

Abstract: Algorithm selection for constraint satisfaction problems requires extracting features that capture problem structure. Manually designing feature extractors demands deep domain expertise and quickly becomes a bottleneck when new problem classes appear. We present an automated approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) in an agentic check--fix--verify loop to synthesize executable Python scripts that act as interpretable, problem-specific feature extractors. Given a high-level MiniZinc model and an instance, the LLM agent generates code that constructs a typed graph representation and computes structural properties such as graph density, variable clustering, and constraint tightness. We evaluate our approach on three combinatorial problems (vehicle routing, car sequencing, fixed-length error-correcting codes) with a portfolio of five state-of-the-art solvers. The synthesized extractors yield algorithm selectors that consistently outperform both expert-curated mzn2feat features (up to $8.3$ percentage points (pp) test-set accuracy on FLECC) and the best transformer-based trans2feat variants. In the meanwhile, the synthesized feature extractors remain inspectable.

</details>


### [105] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了现有大型语言模型(LLM)安全基准是否能有效评估小型语言模型(SLM)。通过对5个广泛使用的基准套件在26个开源SLM上进行大规模评估，发现模糊判断占主导地位，且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关，表明以LLM为中心的安全基准作为SLM安全评估的独立证据是不足的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM在资源受限和隐私敏感的环境中部署越来越广泛，安全和偏见失败可能导致安全和 societal 风险。然而，现有的AI安全/安全/合规基准是为LLM设计的，可能无法可靠地转移到SLM。因此需要研究这些基准是否能有效评估SLM。

Method: 在统一评判标准下(0分有害，1分安全，0.5分模糊/不相关)，评估了5个广泛使用的基准套件在26个开源SLM上的有效性和鲁棒性。

Result: 模糊判断占主导地位，并与提示复杂度和模型架构相关。模糊率随词汇密度、输出困惑度和输出长度增加而增加，随词汇复杂性、自我一致性和回复-提示相似度降低而增加。这揭示了将模型能力与表面安全混淆的能力-安全混淆问题。

Conclusion: 以LLM为中心的安全基准作为SLM安全评估的独立证据是不足的。由于模糊判断普遍存在，聚合平均分排行榜在合理的模糊处理下会显著改变模型排名，即使底层输出保持不变。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {{\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [106] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"decoy hardening"（诱饵强化/Fool's Gold）的防御机制，针对开源大语言模型的安全对齐可被轻易移除的问题。该防御承认拒绝能力会被剥离，但会在攻击状态下注入伪造的错误答案，使攻击者难以获得有效的危险信息。在7个模型（9B-122B，密集型和MoE）上验证，6个模型通过有效性测试，诱饵率达0.51-0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重模型的安全对齐可被"abliteration"等方法在几分钟内移除，现有的发布时防御措施无法持久防止这种攻击。研究动机是探索一种即使在安全对齐被移除后仍能保护模型的方法。

Method: 1）在可微攻击模拟器内训练诱饵，使其仅在攻击状态下表达；2）使用拒绝引脚（refusal pin）和良性牵引（benign leash）保持干净状态下的原始行为；3）在七种模型上测试（9B-122B，包括密集型和MoE架构）。

Result: 6个通过预注册有效性阈值的模型中，攻击状态下对保留提示的响应有0.51-0.90是诱饵（归因增益+0.27-0.84）；所有6个模型保持在良性行为和能力预算内；第7个较小模型未通过阈值。在外部红队基准的CBRNE相关切片上，122B模型在0.82-0.86的匹配质量答案中出现致命错误（未防御时≤0.10）；K=64的逐元素共识在0.083-0.625的提示上重构可用程序（未防御时0.58-0.96）。

Conclusion: 诱饵强化是一种可行的防御策略，可以在安全对齐被移除后继续保护模型；在没有独立 ground truth 的情况下，无法区分伪造答案和正确答案。但该防御不适用于上下文越狱，且仅保护最初发布的防御权重。

Abstract: Safety alignment in open-weight language models is trivially removable: abliteration projects a refusal-mediating direction out of the weights in minutes, and no release-time defense we are aware of prevents it durably. What cannot be prevented can be deceived. Our defense, decoy hardening ("Fool's Gold"), concedes the refusal strip and poisons its payoff: once refusal is stripped, most answers to hazardous operational requests are confident, fluent decoys whose critical elements are falsified. Decoys are trained inside a differentiable simulation of the attack, expressing only in the attacked state; a refusal pin and benign leash hold clean-state behavior to the original. We instantiate it on seven models from five families (9B-122B, dense and mixture-of-experts). On the six models passing our pre-registered efficacy gate, 0.51-0.90 of attacked-state responses to held-out prompts are decoys, +0.27-0.84 attributable to the defense; all six stay within registered benign-behavior and capability budgets; the seventh (smaller) fails the gate (boundary case). Rates replicate on a frozen test split or untouched strata. The claim is epistemic: without independent ground truth, no observation surface we tested separates falsified answers from correct ones - on external red-team benchmarks' CBRNE-adjacent slice, the defended 122B is fatally wrong on 0.82-0.86 of matched-quality answers vs at most 0.10 undefended. Repeated sampling does not restore trust: element-wise consensus at K=64 reconstructs a fully usable procedure on 0.083-0.625 of prompts where the instrument validates, vs 0.58-0.96 undefended, with no label-free way to tell the regimes apart; on the weakest such model the claim is per-draw only. We evaluate chemical and biological hazards; the defense does not address in-context jailbreaks and protects only the initially released defended weights.

</details>


### [107] [Explicit State Elicitation Is Not Enough: A Controlled Audit of Memory-Policy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17247)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Huan Xu,Shuaiting Li,Yiqi Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个针对个性化智能体记忆检索决策的审计协议，评估状态结构化提示、显式状态字段、标签条件分解语义证据等对策略准确性的影响，发现初始合成数据集结果存在词汇可分性偏差，真实控制集上改进有限，且示例级准确率高估了反事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体需要决定如何处理检索到的用户记忆（使用、忽略、更新或查询），但缺乏系统性的审计方法来评估中间输出的有效性。

Method: 开发五步审计协议：审计数据集捷径→分离提示束变化→检查中间标签与答案关联→测试分解语义证据→审计提供商执行失败；使用480示例合成开发集和160示例冻结控制集（含40个四路匹配家族）。

Result: TF-IDF诊断显示词汇可分性，无正面独立忽略案例；显式状态字段对Llama-3.3-70B无显著改善，对GPT-OSS-120B仅有边缘非显著增益；基准关联状态标签改变预测但仅为标签条件诊断；家族级和种子稳定性分析表明示例级准确率高估反事实一致性，四路家族完全成功罕见；分解语义证据未改善路由。

Conclusion: 需要更严格的评估方法防止表面改进；状态字段单独作用有限；标签条件效应不等同于忠实内部机制；示例级指标不足以评估策略一致性。

Abstract: Personalized agents must decide whether retrieved user memory should be used, ignored, updated, or queried before it affects a current task. We use this setting to develop an empirical audit protocol for structured intermediate outputs: first audit dataset shortcuts, then isolate bundled prompt changes, check whether intermediate labels are answer-associated, test decomposed semantic evidence, and audit provider-level execution failures. A 480-example synthetic development set initially suggested large gains from a state-structured prompt bundle, but TF-IDF diagnostics showed lexical separability and no positive standalone Ignore cases. We therefore construct a frozen 160-example controlled counterfactual set with 40 matched four-way families and rule-derived reference policies. On this set, exposing the four state definitions improves accuracy, but an isolated explicit state-output field does not significantly improve policy accuracy for Llama-3.3-70B and gives only a marginal, non-significant gain for GPT-OSS-120B. Supplying benchmark-associated state labels shifts policy predictions, but because those labels deterministically map to policies, this is a label-conditioning diagnostic rather than evidence of a faithful internal mechanism. Family-level and seed-stability analyses further show that example-level accuracy overstates counterfactual consistency: complete four-way family success is rare. An exploratory follow-up that elicits decomposed semantic evidence also fails to improve routing for the cleanly evaluated endpoint; the corresponding GPT-OSS condition was unavailable because of provider-side request validation. We evaluate policy classification only, not downstream responses, tool actions, or memory-store mutation.

</details>


### [108] [Do LLMs Know a Good Hypothesis When They See One? Logit-Based Energy Scoring Outperforms Prompted LLM-as-Judge for Scientific Hypothesis Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17270)
*Swati Rajwal,Sanjay Das,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于logit的能量评分方法，用于评估语言模型生成的科学假设，通过模型内在置信度而非比较判断来评估假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：使用LLM作为评判者或依赖语义相似度，这些方法可能偏好熟悉的观点而忽视新颖的假设。科学假设评估对于可信的AI驱动科学工作流程至关重要。

Method: 提出基于logit的能量评分方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度评估假设。对7个语言模型在12个学科的1323篇论文上进行基准测试，每篇论文配对其假设和15个错误替代选项。

Result: 内在评分达到33.0%的Hit@1，显著优于提示式列表排序的16.6%。最强配置（10亿参数模型结合logit能量评分）达到53.1%的准确率。

Conclusion: 内在模型置信度在科学假设评估中显示出潜力，为可信AI驱动的科学发现提供了基于置信度方法的新研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for scientific hypothesis generation. However, evaluating generated hypotheses remains a challenge for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific workflows. Existing approaches often use LLMs as judges or rely on semantic similarity, which can favor familiar ideas over novel ones. We propose a logit-based energy scoring method that evaluates hypotheses using a language model's intrinsic confidence rather than comparative judgment. We benchmarked seven language models on 1,323 papers across 12 disciplines. Each paper was paired with its hypothesis and fifteen incorrect alternatives. Intrinsic scoring reached 33.0% Hit@1 pooled across both scorers, compared with 16.6% for prompted listwise ranking. The strongest configuration, a 1-billion-parameter model using logit-based energy scoring, reached 53.1%, though this was the maximum across 14 model-by-scorer combinations selected post hoc. Overall, intrinsic model confidence shows potential for scientific hypothesis evaluation. This study also motivates future research on confidence-based methods for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific discovery.

</details>


### [109] [ASI-Bench: At the Dawn of Artificial Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17271)
*Junwei Zhou,Zhen Sun,Binyu Li,Jiangyu Zhou,Yuexi Pan,Hengyu Wang,Honghe Ren,Xiaohan Jia,Xueyang Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Yongchao Chen,Yuanning Feng,Junhao Wu,Cheng Zhang,Sijia Chen,Haoyu Xue,Chengsong You,Huan Wang,Koutian Wu,Peigan Gao,Jiakun Wu,Wenzhe Li,Ergan Shang,Qingyuan Zheng,Jingjing Zhou,Ruixuan Jia,Yan Xu,Hongrui Zhang,Xiao-Han Ma,Zhengxiang Cheng,Yuexing Hao,Liting Mai,Xianglin Ji,Wenjun Zhang,Zhuofan Chen,Yixiao Huang,Chi Wang,Wenyue Hua,Yilun Hao,Yuantao Zhai,Ziyan Zhao,Jingyan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ASI-Bench是首个评估AI系统在通用研究领域中创新探索和自主科学执行能力的基准测试，通过逐步减少人为方法论指导来测试AI独立进行研究的能力。该基准包含60个项目级研究任务横跨11个科学领域，由40多位专家历时31,000+人工小时构建。实验表明，在18种最先进的智能体-模型配置中，平均得分从完全指导的50.91下降到仅指定方法的29.10，再到自主确定方法的26.62，显示当前系统仍高度依赖人类指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统的能力仍主要建立在学习、压缩和应用现有人类知识的基础上，现有基准主要测试AI是否能够基于学习知识产生正确答案或在广泛人类指导下完成任务。ASI需要AI超越现有知识，探索未知、创造新知识并将新想法转化为可验证结果，但尚无基准评估AI在这些方面的能力。

Method: 构建ASI-Bench基准，包含60个项目级研究任务横跨11个科学领域，逐步减少方法论指导（从完全指导到仅指定方法再到自主确定方法），由40多位专家历经31,000+人工小时完成。所有任务经过专家审查、AI辅助审计、沙箱执行和评分器验证。对18种最先进的智能体-模型配置进行测试。

Result: 平均得分从50.91（完全方法论指导）下降到29.10（仅指定方法）和26.62（自主确定方法）。这一显著下降表明当前系统仍严重依赖人类指导，距离自主完成端到端项目级科学研究还很遥远。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统仍高度依赖人类指导，距离实现人工超级智能的自主科学研究能力还有很大差距。ASI-Bench为评估AI的创新探索和自主科学执行能力提供了首个标准化基准，公开供全球研究人员和开发者使用，以推动向人工超级智能的集体进步。

Abstract: Artificial superintelligence (ASI) requires AI to move beyond mastering existing knowledge toward exploring the unknown, creating new knowledge, and turning new ideas into verifiable results. However, the capabilities of today's AI systems are still largely built on learning, compressing, and applying existing human knowledge. Accordingly, existing benchmarks primarily test whether AI can produce correct answers based on learned knowledge, or whether it can complete tasks under extensive human guidance. We therefore introduce ASI-Bench, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate AI systems' capabilities of innovative exploration and autonomous scientific execution across general research domains, and the first to progressively withdraw human methodological guidance within the same research project to test how far AI can proceed on its own. Built by over 40 experts with the cost of 31,000+ human hours, ASI-Bench contains 60 project-level research tasks across 11 scientific domains and progressively reduces methodological guidance to test whether AI can independently select methods, conduct research, and produce verifiable results. All tasks undergo expert review, AI-assisted auditing, sandbox execution, and scorer validation. Across 18 state-of-the-art agent--model configurations, the average score drops from 50.91 with full methodological guidance to 29.10 with only the method specified and 26.62 when agents must determine the method themselves. This sharp decline shows that current systems remain heavily dependent on human guidance and are still far from autonomously conducting end-to-end, project-level scientific research. ASI-Bench is open to the world. We invite researchers and builders everywhere to contribute new tasks, challenge the limits of today's AI, and help accelerate humanity's collective path toward artificial superintelligence at https://asibench.apexin.ai/submit.

</details>


### [110] [DeAR: Decentralized Agentic Reasoning via Capability Grounding and Collaborative Thought Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17282)
*Xing Wei,Changmeng Zheng,XiaoYong Wei,Xiufen Ye,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeAR (Decentralized Agentic Reasoning) 是一个去中心化的多智能体推理框架，通过对等协作取代传统的中心化协议，旨在解决复杂多模态查询处理中的路由瓶颈和静态角色分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推理系统依赖中心化协议，导致路由瓶颈和静态角色分配，无法有效处理复杂的多模态查询。

Method: 提出三个核心机制：(1) 去中心化能力锚定，实现查询依赖的智能体专业化；(2) 思维图导航，实现有针对性的对等交互；(3) 拓扑更新，实现自适应错误修正。

Result: 在9个不同的多模态推理和文本问答基准测试中，DeAR始终优于最近的基线方法。

Conclusion: 去中心化和自适应协作可增强知识密集型推理任务的准确性。

Abstract: Existing agentic reasoning systems typically rely on centralized protocols. This design introduces routing bottlenecks and static role allocations that often fail when handling complex multimodal queries. We propose DeAR (Decentralized Agentic Reasoning), a framework that shifts from central control to autonomous peer-to-peer collaboration.
